&\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0}}L}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)-d(0)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\right|\right]\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt+C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.\end{split}

(E.12)

To evaluate the remaining expectation, first note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)-d(0)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\right|\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)-d(0)\right|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt+\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}d(0)\left|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\right|\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)-d(0)\right|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)+\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}d(0)\left|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(0)\right|,\end{split}

(E.13)

where the last line again uses that
d(z)\geq d(0)
on
J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})
and the definition of
b_{0,k}(z)
. For the first summand, we estimate

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)-d(0)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|\frac{2z\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-2\rho_{0}\right)+\frac{z^{2}(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left(2Kn^{-(1-\gamma)/2}\left|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-2\rho_{0}\right|+\frac{K^{2}}{n}\right).

Then by an analogous computation as used in (
E.7
) with a subsequent application of Corollary
B.1
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|d(z)-d(0)\right|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}.

(E.14)

As
ab\leq(a+b)^{2}
for
ab>0
we find

\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}d(0)\leq\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\frac{2}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-2\rho_{0}\right)^{2}

and then with an analogous reasoning as in (
E.7
) with a subsequent application of Corollary
B.1
we find for the second summand in the right-hand side of (
E.13
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}d(0)|\left|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(0)\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}.

(E.15)

Plugging in the estimates (
E.14
) and (
E.15
) into (
E.12
) yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\left|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(0)-b_{n,k}(z)+b_{n,k}(0)\right|\right]\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)}{n\sqrt{k}}

such that because
|a_{n}|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|S_{n,k}^{1,2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

(E.16)

\mathbf{-\ S_{n,k}^{1,3}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})}
. Recall
d(z)
given in (
E.9
). Inserting the the Taylor expansion (
E.10
) for
b_{n,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(z)
and
b_{n,k}(0)-b_{0,k}(0)
and subsequently applying the triangle inequality reveals

\displaystyle\left|b_{n,k}(z)b_{0,k}(0)-b_{0,k}(z)b_{n,k}(0)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\left|b_{0,k}(0)\left(b_{n,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(z)\right)-b_{0,k}(z)\left(b_{n,k}(0)-b_{0,k}(0)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\left|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\left[\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)d(z)-\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)d(0)\right]\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)d(0)\left[\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)\right]\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\frac{1}{2}d(z)^{2}\exp\left(-\xi_{1}(z)d(z)\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\frac{1}{2}d(0)^{2}\exp\left(-\xi_{2}d(0)\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right|,

for two intermediate values
\xi_{1}(z),\xi_{2}
between
\alpha_{0}^{-2}
and
\alpha_{n}^{-2}
. Taking into account that
d(z)\geq 0
and therefore
|\exp(-\alpha_{0}^{-2}d(z))|\leq 1
on
J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})
for all
z\in\mathbb{R}
, the last two terms can be treated analogously as the remainder in
S_{n,k}^{1,2}
and yield the same bound (
E.11
). The square bracket in the second summand equals
b_{0,k}(0)-b_{0,k}(z)
and the square bracket within the first summand equals the left-hand side of (
E.13
) and is in particular bounded as in (
E.13
). This means that in total the first two summands are bounded by

\displaystyle\left[\left|d(z)-d(0)\right|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)+d(0)\left|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{0,k}(0)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\left(1+d(0)\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(0)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right)

Finally, we use
(1+d(0))\exp(-\alpha_{0}^{-2}d(0))\leq C_{\alpha_{0}}
,
|a_{0}a_{n}|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
and the two uniform moment bounds (
E.14
) and (
E.15
) and arrive at

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|S_{n,k}^{1,3}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

(E.17)

Summing up the bounds (
E.8
), (
E.16
) and (
E.17
), one obtains

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|S_{n,k}^{1}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\rightarrow 0.

\mathbf{\blacktriangleright S_{n}^{2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})}
. Using
x/(1+x)\leq\log(1+x)\leq x
for
x>-1
we obtain (analogously to
S_{n,k}^{1}
)

\displaystyle\left|\log\left(\frac{1-a_{n}b_{n,k}(z)}{1-a_{0}b_{0,k}(z)}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left|a_{0}b_{0,k}(z)-a_{n}b_{n,k}(z)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(|a_{0}-a_{n}||b_{0,k}(z)|+|a_{n}||b_{0,k}(z)-b_{n,k}(z)|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(|a_{0}-a_{n}|+|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{n,k}(z)|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})},

where the last step uses that
|b_{0,k}(z)|\leq 1
on
J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})\supset J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})
and uniform boundedness of
|a_{n}|
for
|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}
. It is clear by (
E.6
) that

\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|a_{0}-a_{n}\right|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}

and by a first order Taylor expansion we find

\displaystyle\left|b_{0,k}(z)-b_{n,k}(z)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}d(z)\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}d(z)\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right|d(z)\exp\left(-\xi(z)d(z)\right)\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left|\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right|,

for some intermediate value
\xi(z)
between
\alpha_{0}^{-2}
and
\alpha_{n}^{-2}
. The last step uses boundedness of
x\mapsto x\exp(-x^{2}/c)
the fact that
\xi(z)
is uniformly (in
z
) bounded from below by a positive constant for
n
large enough which we also tacitly assume in the sequel. Summing up these to estimates gives

\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}\left|\log\left(\frac{1-a_{n}b_{n,k}(z)}{1-a_{0}b_{0,k}(z)}\right)\right|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

Then by definition of
S_{n}^{2}
in (
E.5
) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|S_{n}^{2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{1,k}(\rho_{0})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\rightarrow 0,

where the last step follows as for
T_{n}^{1,1}
.

\bullet\ \underline{m=3}:
Here, we have the decomposition

\displaystyle T_{n}^{3}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle=T_{n}^{3,1}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})+T_{n}^{3,2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})+T_{n}^{3,3}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})+T_{n}^{3,4}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n}),

where

\displaystyle T_{n}^{3,1}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle:=\log\left(\frac{(\alpha_{n}\beta_{0}(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{n}(\alpha_{n}+\beta_{n})}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right),

\displaystyle T_{n}^{3,2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right)\left(\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.-\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\beta_{n}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha_{n}}\right)^{2}\right),

\displaystyle T_{n}^{3,3}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle:=\frac{z\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{0}}\right)\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt-\left.\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{n}}\right)\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\beta_{n}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha_{n}}\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})},

\displaystyle T_{n}^{3,4}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle:=\frac{(z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{0}}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{n}}\right)^{2}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}.

First of all, we note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right|\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.\end{split}

(E.18)

As in (
E.2
), we obtain from this

\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0},\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{z\in[0,K]}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}KL\frac{1}{\sqrt{kn}}.

\mathbf{-\ T_{n}^{3,1}}
.

By a Taylor expansion, we obtain

\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|\log\left(\frac{(\alpha_{n}\beta_{0}(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{n}(\alpha_{n}+\beta_{n})}\right)\right|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

Together with (
E.18
), we then obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|T_{n}^{3,1}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}LK\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\rightarrow 0.

\mathbf{-\ T_{n}^{3,2}}
.

We first bound

\left|T_{n}^{3,2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\leq R_{n}^{1}(z,\beta_{n})+R_{n}^{2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})+R_{n}^{3}(z,\alpha_{n}),

where

\displaystyle R_{n}^{1}(z,\beta_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\left|\frac{1}{\beta_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{n}^{2}}\right|\left|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle R_{n}^{2}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\left|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\left|\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}\beta_{n}}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\left|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle R_{n}^{3}(z,\alpha_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\left|\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}^{2}}\right|\left|\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0}+z\psi_{n}^{\gamma})}-\mathbbm{1}_{J_{3,k}(\rho_{0})}\right|.

By a Taylor argument and using the bound (
E.18
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|R_{n}^{1}(z,\beta_{n})\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{C_{\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\frac{C_{\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

Using the condition of the indicator, the second summand can be directly bounded as

\frac{C_{\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\leq C_{\beta_{0}}LK^{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},

which converges to zero as
\sqrt{n}(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}(\Delta_{n}^{\gamma})^{-1}=n^{-1/2}
. For the first one, we estimate its expectation. Using the bound (
B.1
) on the transition density, this gives first for a single summand

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\textasciiacute)^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\right]\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}\}}\int_{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\frac{(y-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\right.\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}\}}\left(\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+1\right)\right]\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(1+y^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(K^{2}\frac{(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}\right),\end{split}

(E.19)

where the last step uses the bound (
E.2
). Hence, we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{C_{\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\left(K^{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}\right)\rightarrow 0

and thus we have shown

\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|R_{n}^{1}(z,\beta_{n})\right|\right]\rightarrow 0.

The term
R_{n}^{3}
can be dealt with analogously. For
R_{n}^{2}
we again apply a Taylor argument and (
E.18
) to obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|R_{n}^{2}(z,\beta_{n})\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\left|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\left|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

Next, we bound the expectation of a single summand using the bound (
B.1
) on the transition density. Analogously to (
E.19
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{|X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}|}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\frac{\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}|}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma},X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}|}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\left.\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}|}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\frac{K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\frac{K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\frac{K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}},

where the last step follows by Corollary
B.1
. In total, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|S_{n}^{2}(z,\beta_{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\frac{\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}+\frac{K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{n}+K+1\right)\rightarrow 0.

\mathbf{-\ T_{n}^{3,3}}
.

This can be done analogously to the treatment of
R_{n}^{2}
in the step
T_{n}^{3,2}
before.

\mathbf{-\ T_{n}^{3,4}}
.

By a Taylor expansion, we find

\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|\left(\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{0}}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{n}}-\frac{1}{\beta_{n}}\right)^{2}\right)\right|\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

Using the explicit form of
\psi_{n}^{\gamma}
and bounding each indicator by one yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sup_{z\in[-K,K]}\sup_{|\alpha_{n}-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta_{n}-\beta_{0}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}}\left|T_{n}^{3,4}(z,\alpha_{n},\beta_{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}LK^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{(\psi_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}n=C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}LK^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\rightarrow 0.

F
Details on the proof of Proposition
3.3

This section is split into three parts. In Subsection
F.1
, we provide all details left out in the sketch of proof in Section
7
, except for Lemma
7.2
which is proven in Subsection
F.2
. Finally, Subsection
F.3
contains additional auxiliary results that are used within Subsection
F.1
.

F.1
Details for Section
7

We start with a small remark that explains why (
7.5
) is valid. Thereafter, the rest of the details left out in Section
7
is given.

Remark F.1
.

Lemma
4.1
in
Brutsche and Rohde 2026
remains valid for
K,L\geq 0
. In this case, we obtain the moment bounds

\mathbb{E}[F_{n}^{1}(K,L)n^{-1/2}]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}[F_{n}^{2}(K,L)n^{-2/3}]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)<\infty
for the remainders
F_{n}^{1}
and
F_{n}^{2}
, where in case
L=0
the constant
C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(L)
is replaced by
C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
. The proof of this does not change at all. Here, we no longer have the statement that these moment bounds scale linearly in
L
, but the decomposition (
7.4
) does not involve
L
and hence we do not need such a statement for the remainder in (
7.5
).

In what follows, we are going to prove that for an appropriate choice of
K=(K_{1},K_{2},K_{3})\in(0,\infty)^{3}
,

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\in\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{j}(K)}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\geq 0,B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\right)<\epsilon,

(F.1)

for
j=1,2,3
following the route of proof given in Section
7
. The event
E^{\gamma}
is given in (
7.1
) and
B_{n}
is chosen to be the intersection of the events specified in Lemma
7.2
, Lemma
F.4
and Lemma
D.2
, where the latter is replaced by Lemma
4.1
in
Brutsche and Rohde 2026
in case
\gamma=1
. Throughout the proof, we use the notation

f(x)=\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)\quad\textrm{ and }\quad g(x)=|\log(x)|+\frac{1}{2}\left|x^{2}-1\right|.

Note that
f(x)\leq 0
with equality if and only if
x=1
and
f(x)\rightarrow-\infty
for both
x\to 0
and
x\to\infty
. During the proof, we will enumerate constants
C_{j}
for an easier exposition. Moreover, we explicitly highlight their dependence on the parameters
K_{1},K_{2},K_{3}
that have to be specified. Besides, the constants depend on
\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}
and the constants
\Gamma,\xi,\zeta
appearing in (
7.2
) which is suppressed in notation.

Although the constant
K=(K_{1},K_{2},K_{3})
has to be specified first for
j=3
(which only depens on
K_{3}
), then for
j=2
(that depends on
K_{2},K_{3}
) and finally for
j=1
(which depends on
K_{1},K_{2},K_{3})
, we provide the derivation of (
F.1
) in reversed order. The reason is that this order leads to a significantly more transparent proof.

\bullet\ \mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{1}(K)
.
We assume
K_{2},K_{3}
to be given and construct
K_{1}
. By Lemma
7.2
(a) with
L=K_{2}
and Lemma
7.2
(d) we have the bound

\sup_{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\in\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{1}}\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq C(K_{2})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

for
n
sufficiently large, where we have used a Taylor expansion of
g
in
x_{0}=1
in the application of Lemma
7.2
(d) which reveals
g(\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})),g(\beta_{0}/(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}))\leq C(K_{2})/\sqrt{n}
for
|\theta_{\alpha}|,|\theta_{\beta}|\leq K_{2}/\sqrt{n}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\in\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{1}(K)}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\geq 0,B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{K_{1}n^{-(1+\gamma)/2}<|\theta_{\rho}|<K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}}\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})\geq-C(K_{2}),B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\right).

With this, we are back in the proof of consistency for known
\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}
. In particular, showing that this term is bounded by
\epsilon
for
K_{1}
large enough is obtained for
\gamma=1
by following the steps from
(4.16)
onwards in Subsection
4.2
of
Brutsche and Rohde 2026
. For
\gamma\in(0,1)
it works the same and is part of the proof of Lemma
D.2
.

\bullet\ \mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{2}(K)
.
We assume
K_{3}
in
K=(K_{1},K_{2},K_{3})\in(0,\infty)^{3}
to be given and construct
K_{2}
. Note that
T_{n,\gamma}^{2}(K)
does not depend on
K_{1}
. For constants
C^{\prime},K^{\prime}
we first estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\Bigg(\sup_{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\in\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{2}(K)}\ell_{n}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\geq 0,B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\Bigg(\sup_{K^{\prime}n^{-(1+\gamma)/2}\leq|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}}\ell_{n}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})\geq 0,B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\Bigg)

(F.2)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathbb{P}_{0}\Bigg(\sup_{|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K^{\prime}n^{-(1+\gamma)/2}}|\ell_{n}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})|\geq C^{\prime},E^{\gamma}\Bigg)

(F.3)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+2\mathbb{P}_{0}\Bigg(\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\max\{|\theta_{\alpha}|,|\theta_{\beta}|\}>K_{2}/\sqrt{n}\\
|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\end{subarray}}\ell_{n}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\geq-C^{\prime},B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\Bigg).

(F.4)

To proceed, we first choose
K^{\prime}
large enough such that (
F.2
) is bounded by
\epsilon
. This is possible as already outlined in the discussion of
\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{1}
. For this
K^{\prime}
, choose
C^{\prime}
large enough such that
\limsup_{n\to\infty}\eqref{eq_proof:P2}<\epsilon
. By Theorem
13.2
in
Billingsley 1999
, this is possible as
(\ell_{n}^{1}(zn^{-(1+\gamma)/2}))_{[-K^{\prime},K^{\prime}]}
converges towards a tight limit in the Skorohod space
\mathcal{D}([-K^{\prime},K^{\prime}])
by (1A) for
\gamma\in(0,1)
and by Proposition
1.2
in
Brutsche and Rohde 2026
for
\gamma=1
.
It is therefore sufficient to deal with (
F.4
) for given
C^{\prime}
. By (b)-(d) of Lemma
7.2
we find for any
R>0
a constant
C_{1}=C_{1}(K_{3},R)>0
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}&\leq\bigg(\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(0,\theta_{\alpha})+\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{>}(0,\theta_{\beta})+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}\right)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\left.+C_{1}\left(g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\sqrt{n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}},\end{split}

(F.5)

where the constant
d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}>0
is explicitly given in Lemma
7.2
(d). Recalling
f(x)\leq 0
and applying Lemma
7.1
for the parameters
a=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
and
b=0
yields

\displaystyle\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(0,\theta_{\alpha})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{2}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)

(F.6)

for
C_{2}=\xi\zeta/\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}
and with
a=0
,
b=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
we also obtain

\displaystyle\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{>}(0,\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{2}nf\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right).

(F.7)

This means that

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\Bigg(\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\max\{|\theta_{\alpha}|,|\theta_{\beta}|\}>K_{2}/\sqrt{n}\\
|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\end{subarray}}\ell_{n}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\geq-C^{\prime},B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\Bigg)=0

follows by the estimates (
F.5
), (
F.6
) and (
F.7
) once we show that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\max\{|\theta_{\alpha}|,|\theta_{\beta}|\}>K_{2}/\sqrt{n}\\
|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\end{subarray}}\left(C_{2}n\left(f\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+f\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\right.\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\left.+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}\right)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}\left(g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\right)<-C^{\prime}\end{split}

(F.8)

for all
n\geq n_{0}
with
n_{0}
large enough, appropriate
R>0
and
K_{2}
sufficiently large. Note that the restriction for
\theta_{\alpha}
and
\theta_{\beta}
does not prevent the terms
g(\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}))
and
g(\beta_{0}/(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}))
from exploding. However, these terms appear as a multiple of
\sqrt{n}
, compared to the leading terms involving the negative function
f
that contain the factor
n
and indeed turn out to compensate for the remainder involving
g
for any
\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}
.

We continue with proving (
F.8
). Here, we first use
f(x)\rightarrow-\infty
for
x\to 0
in order to get rid of the term
nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}
. To this aim, choose
0<r<1
small enough such that for
x\leq r
we have
f(x)<-2d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}/C_{2}
. Then,

\displaystyle C_{2}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\leq\frac{C_{2}n}{2}f\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\quad\textrm{ for }\ \theta_{\alpha}>\frac{(1-r)\alpha_{0}}{r}.

An analogous estimate holds true when replacing
\alpha_{0}
by
\beta_{0}
and
\theta_{\alpha}
by
\theta_{\beta}
such that for the choice

\displaystyle R:=\frac{1-r}{r}\max\{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}\}

(F.9)

a sufficient condition for (
F.8
) is given by

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\max\{|\theta_{\alpha}|,|\theta_{\beta}|\}>K_{2}/\sqrt{n}\\
|\theta_{\rho}|\leq K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\end{subarray}}\sqrt{n}\left(F_{n,2C_{1}/C_{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+F_{n,2C_{1}/C_{2}}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)<-2C^{\prime}/C_{2},

for all
n\geq n_{0}
and
K_{2}
sufficiently large, where the function
F_{n,C}
is given as

\displaystyle F_{n,C}(x)=\sqrt{n}\left(\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)\right)+C\left|\log(x)\right|+\frac{C}{2}\left|x^{2}-1\right|.

(F.10)

Such a choice of
K_{2}
is possible by properties (a) and (b) of
F_{n,C}
given in Lemma
F.3
.

\bullet\ \mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3}(K)
.
As outlined in Section
7
we have to combine effects of
\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}
and
\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}
. One crucial point is to understand the effect of the term
T_{n,\gamma}
in the decomposition (
7.7
). The stochastic order of this term is determined by the number of indices
k
with
\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
and therefore we need to distinguish further cases. Moreover, there appears another special case: If
\theta_{\rho}
is chosen such that the number of indices
k
for which
X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
is no longer of order
n
, the negative compensator of the term
g(\beta_{0}/(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}))
is missing. This term emerges from an application of Lemma
7.2
(d) and explodes as
\theta_{\beta}\to-\beta_{0}
and
\theta_{\beta}\to\infty
. For these boundary cases, we therefore no longer use the decomposition (
7.7
) together with Lemma
7.2
as we did for
\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{2}(K)
. Instead, we will base our analysis for
\theta_{\beta}\approx-\beta
and
\theta_{\beta}\to\infty
on the decomposition in Lemma
F.4
.

To make the above mentioned heuristics precise, we fix
\delta_{l},\delta_{u}>0
and recall
\zeta>0
from the definition of the event
A_{n}
in (
7.2
) that was chosen such that
(X_{t})_{t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
has crossed the value
\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
at least once. We then decompose

\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3}(K)=\bigcup_{m=1}^{5}\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,m}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

with the disjoint sets

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,1}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

\displaystyle:=\left\{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}):K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}<\theta_{\rho}<n^{1/4-\gamma/2}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,2}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

\displaystyle:=\left\{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}):n^{1/4-\gamma/2}\leq\theta_{\rho}<\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,3}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

\displaystyle:=\left\{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}):\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2\leq\theta_{\rho}<\infty,\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}<\delta_{l}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,4}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

\displaystyle:=\left\{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}):\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2\leq\theta_{\rho}<\infty,\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,5}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})

\displaystyle:=\left\{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}):\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2\leq\theta_{\rho}<\infty,\delta_{l}\leq\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}\leq\delta_{u}\right\}.

In what follows we bound the probability

\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\in\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,m}(K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u})}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\geq 0,B_{n}\cap E^{\gamma}\right)

by
\epsilon
for
m=1,2,3,4,5
,
n\geq n_{0}
and appropriate
K_{3},\delta_{l},\delta_{u}
. Hereby, it is important to choose
\delta_{l},\delta_{u}
such that the probability for
m=3,4
satisfies the required bound and then afterwards prove that for this choice the term for
m=5
can also be bounded by
\epsilon
for
n
sufficiently large.

\underline{m=1}
: Parts (c) and (d) of Lemma
7.2
together with the bound (
7.5
) yield for any
R>0
and a constant
C_{3}=C_{3}(R)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta_{\rho}\in[K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2},n^{1/4-\gamma/2})}R_{n,\gamma}^{all}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{3}\sqrt{n}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}\right)

where the constant
d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
is explicitly given in Lemma
7.2
(d). Applying Lemma
7.1
to the parameters
a=-n^{1/4-\gamma/2}
,
b=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
yields for a constant
C_{4}\leq C_{2}=\xi\zeta/\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}\in[Kn^{-\gamma/2},n^{1/4-\gamma/2})}\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{>}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq-\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{4}nf\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right).

Inserting these two estimates and (
F.6
) into the decompostion (
7.7
) then gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{4}n\left(f\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+f\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{3}\sqrt{n}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right).\end{split}

(F.11)

We recall at this point that

T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})=H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

for the function

H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})=\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-1\right)+\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}

for which by Lemma
F.2
we have
H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})\leq 0
with equality if and only if
\theta_{\alpha}=\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}
. Compared to (
F.8
), the supremum is taken over
\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{3,1}
instead of
\mathcal{T}_{n,\gamma}^{1}
and we have the additional terms
T_{n,\gamma}
and
C_{3}\sqrt{n}
, where the first one is negative and the second one an additional remainder. The crucial observation is now that
T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})
and
f(\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}))
which are both negative cannot be close to zero at the same time as for the first expression this happens for
\theta_{\alpha}\approx\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}
and for the second one in case
\theta_{\alpha}\approx 0
. To make this precise, we first choose
R
as in (
F.9
) and then further bound the right-hand side of (
F.11
) (recall
f(x)\leq 0
) by

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left[\frac{C_{4}\sqrt{n}}{2}\left(F_{n,2C_{3}/C_{4}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+F_{n,2C_{3}/C_{4}}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{3}\sqrt{n}\right].

We now distinguish two cases and abbreviate
\kappa=|\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}|/4
:

(1)\ \mathbf{\theta_{\alpha}\in B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}
.

Since in this case
|\theta_{\alpha}|>\kappa
, which means
\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})\notin B_{c(\kappa)}(1)
for some constant
c(\kappa)>0
. Then by Lemma
F.3
(a) and (c), and
T_{n,\gamma}\leq 0
, we obtain for some constant
C_{5}=C_{5}(\kappa)
,

\displaystyle\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq-\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{5}n+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{3}\sqrt{n},

where the right-hand side is independent of
\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha}
and
\theta_{\beta}
and clearly converges to
-\infty
for
n\to\infty
.

(2)\ \mathbf{\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}
.

In this case for
\theta_{\alpha}
, there exists a constant
C_{6}=C_{6}(\kappa)
such that
H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})\leq-C_{6}
. As by Lemma
F.3
(a)
\sqrt{n}F_{n,C}
is bounded, we find some constant
C_{7}
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\leq\theta_{\rho}<n^{1/4-\gamma/2}}\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\delta(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{7}\sqrt{n}-C_{6}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\}}.

On the event

B_{n}^{\prime}:=\left\{\frac{C_{6}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+K_{3}n^{-\gamma/2}\}}>2C_{7}\right\}

this upper bound converges to
-\infty
for
n\to\infty
. Taking
K_{3}>0
large enough ensures
\inf_{n\geq n_{0}}\mathbb{P}_{0}(B_{n}^{\prime})>1-\epsilon
for appropriate
n_{0}
by Lemma
D.1
.

\underline{m=2}
: Here, part (c) and (d) of Lemma
7.2
together with the bound (
7.5
) yield for any
R>0
and a constant
C_{8}=C_{8}(R)
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>n^{1/4-\gamma/2}}R_{n,\gamma}^{all}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}&\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{8}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}\right).\end{split}

(F.12)

Next, Lemma
7.1
for the parameters
a=-\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2
and
b=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
yields for the constant
C_{9}=\xi\zeta/(2\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\})

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}\in(n^{1/4-\gamma/2},\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2)}\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{>}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{9}nf\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right).

Let
R
be chosen as in (
F.9
). By Lemma
F.3
(a) we obtain for a constant
C_{10}=C_{10}(R)
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&C_{8}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)+C_{9}nf\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\beta}>R\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{C_{9}\sqrt{n}}{2}F_{n,(2C_{8}/C_{9})n^{\gamma/6}}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\leq C_{10}n^{\gamma/3}\end{split}

(F.13)

and with (
F.6
) it follows from the decomposition (
7.7
) that

\displaystyle\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(C_{2}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{8}n^{1/2/+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+(C_{8}+C_{10})n^{2/3}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\frac{C_{2}\sqrt{n}}{2}F_{n,(2C_{8}/C_{2})n^{\gamma/6}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+(C_{8}+C_{10})n^{2/3}+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\right),

where the last step uses again that
R
was chosen as in (
F.9
). We now distiguish cases, similar as we did in the study of
m=1
. We set
\kappa=|\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}|/4
.

(1)\ \mathbf{\theta_{\alpha}\in B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}
.

Since
|\theta_{\alpha}|>\kappa
, which means
\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})\notin B_{c(\kappa)}(1)
for some constant
c(\kappa)>0
. Then by Lemma
F.3
(c), and
T_{n,\gamma}\leq 0
, we obtain for some constant
C_{11}=C_{11}(\kappa)
,

\displaystyle\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(-C_{11}n+(C_{8}+C_{10})n^{2/3}\right),

where the right-hand side is independent of
\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha}
and
\theta_{\beta}
and converges to zero for
n\to\infty
.

(2)\ \mathbf{\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}
.

As in (2) for
m=1
, there exists the constant
C_{6}=C_{6}(\kappa)
such that
H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})\leq-C_{6}
. Using Lemma
7.1
with
a=0
and
b=n^{1/4-\gamma/2}
gives

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>n^{1/4-\gamma/2}}\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq-\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\frac{C_{6}\xi}{\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}n^{3/4}.

Repeating the estimate (
F.13
) for
\alpha_{0}
instead of
\beta_{0}
and
C_{2}
instead of
C_{9}
then yields for another constant
C_{12}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>n^{1/4-\gamma/2}}\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\kappa(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left((C_{8}+C_{10}+C_{12})n^{2/3}-\frac{C_{6}\xi}{\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}n^{3/4}\right),

which converges to
-\infty
for
n\to\infty
.

\underline{m=3}
: We want to apply Lemma
F.4
and denote the zwo constants on its right-hand side by
C_{13}
and
C_{14}
. Let
r
be small enough such that
f(x)<-2d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}/C_{13}
for
x\leq r
, then for
R=\alpha_{0}(1-r)/r
we find

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(C_{13}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{14}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{14}n^{2/3}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\frac{C_{13}n}{2}f\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{14}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{14}n^{2/3}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\frac{C_{13}\sqrt{n}}{2}F_{n,(2C_{14}/C_{13})n^{\gamma/6}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{14}n^{2/3}\right).

Applying Lemma
7.1
to
a=0
and
b=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2
yields for the already introduced constant
C_{9}=\xi\zeta/(2\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\})
,

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2}T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\leq C_{9}H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})n.

Let
\kappa=|\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}|/4
. Distinguishing cases for
\theta_{\alpha}
as was already done for
m=2
then yields
F_{n,(2C_{14}/C_{13})n^{\gamma/6}}(\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}))\leq-C_{15}n
in case
\theta_{\alpha}\in B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})
and
H_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\theta_{\alpha})<-C_{6}
for
\theta_{\alpha}\notin B_{\kappa}(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})
such that we have in total

\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\frac{C_{13}\sqrt{n}}{2}F_{n,(2C_{14}/C_{13})n^{\gamma/6}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{14}n^{2/3}\right)\leq-\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{16}n

for some constant
C_{13}>0
. Then, Lemma
F.4
(i) yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l})}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\geq 0\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l})}A_{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})>C_{16}n\right),

where

\displaystyle A_{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left[2\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right].

To bound the remaining probability we first derive a deterministic upper bound in order to get rid of both suprema and subsequently apply Markov’s inequality. To this aim, define
S_{j}(\delta_{l})=(-\beta_{0}+2^{-(j+1)}\delta_{l},-\beta_{0}+2^{-j}\delta_{l})
. For
x\geq 1
,

2\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)y\geq 0\quad\Leftrightarrow\quad y\leq\frac{4\log(x)}{x^{2}-1},

where the upper bound is a decreasing function for
x\to\infty
and should be read as
\infty
in case
x=1
. Hence, we obtain for any
\delta_{l}<\beta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l}]}\sum_{k=1}^{n}A_{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})&\leq\sum_{j\geq 0}\sup_{\theta_{\rho}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in S_{j}(\delta_{l})}A_{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})\\
&\leq\sum_{j\geq 0}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{2^{-(j+1)}\delta_{l}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{k,j}(\delta_{l})},\end{split}

(F.14)

where

A_{k,j}(\delta_{l})=\left\{\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\leq\frac{4\log(\beta_{0}/(2^{-j}\delta_{l}))}{(\beta_{0}/(2^{-j}\delta_{l}))^{2}-1}\right\}.

Note that this bound does not include a supremum over
\theta_{\rho}
or
\theta_{\beta}
any longer. As the upper bound in
A_{k,j}(\delta_{l})
is small for large values of
j
and
(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}/\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}
has an expectation bounded away from zero, we expect the probability of
A_{k,j}(\delta_{l})
to be small for large
j
. Indeed, for any
a>0
, we obtain with the upper bound (
B.1
) on the transition density,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\leq a\right)\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt=\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}a}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}a}\right)\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\int_{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}a}}^{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}+\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}a}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)dy\right]\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\int_{-\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}a}}^{\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}a}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)dy\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sqrt{a}.\end{split}

(F.15)

By the bound (
F.14
), Markov’s inequality and (
F.15
), we obtain for
\delta_{l}
small enough

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l})}A_{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})>C_{16}n\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{C_{16}n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sum_{j\geq 0}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{2^{-(j+1)}\delta_{l}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{A_{k,j}(\delta_{l})}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{j\geq 0}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{2^{-(j+1)}\delta_{l}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{4\log(\beta_{0}/(2^{-j}\delta_{l}))}{(\beta_{0}/(2^{-j}\delta_{l}))^{2}-1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\delta_{l}+\delta_{l}\log(1/\delta_{l})^{3/2}\right)\sum_{j>0}j^{3/2}2^{-j}.

As the series
\sum_{j>0}j^{3/2}2^{-j}
converges and
x\log(1/x)\rightarrow 0
for
x\searrow 0
, the last expression is smaller than
\epsilon
if
\delta_{l}
is chosen small enough.

\underline{m=4}
: Repeating the first steps of the case
m=3
and using Lemma
F.4
(ii) reveals

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}-\beta_{0}}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\geq 0\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}-\beta_{0}}A_{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})\geq C_{16}n\right),

where for
d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
given explicitly in Lemma
7.2
(d),

\displaystyle A_{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}+d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\right].

For
\delta_{u}
large enough such that
\log(\beta_{0}/\delta_{u})+d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}<\log(\beta_{0}/\delta_{u})/2
, we obtain the estimates

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}-\beta_{0}}A_{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\delta_{u}}\right)+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{k}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\delta_{u}}\right)+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right],

where

E_{k}:=\left\{\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}>\log\left(\frac{\delta_{u}}{\beta_{0}}\right)\right\}.

Note that
E_{k}=\bigcup_{j=1}^{\infty}E_{k,j},
where

E_{k,j}=\left\{j\log\left(\frac{\delta_{u}}{\beta_{0}}\right)<\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\leq(j+1)\log\left(\frac{\delta_{u}}{\beta_{0}}\right)\right\}.

By Markov’s inequality and Corollary
B.2
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(E_{k,j}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\leq(j+1)\log\left(\frac{\delta_{u}}{\beta_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{(j+1)\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})}\right)^{3}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{n}^{\gamma})^{3}\beta_{0}^{6}}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)^{6}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{(j+1)\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})}\right)^{3}.

Applying the union bound then reveals

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}}/2}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}-\beta_{0}}A_{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})\geq C_{16}n\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{k}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\delta_{u}}\right)+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]>C_{16}n\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{k,j}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\delta_{u}}\right)+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]>2C_{16}n\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{0}\left((j-1)\log\left(\frac{\delta_{u}}{\beta_{0}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{k,j}}>2C_{16}n\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{j\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})}{2C_{16}n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{E_{k,j+1}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{j\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})}{2C_{16}n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{(j+2)\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\frac{1}{2C_{16}\log(\delta_{u}/\beta_{0})^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{j^{2}}.

As the series converges, we can choose
\delta_{u}
large enough such that the whole expression is bounded by
\epsilon
.

\underline{m=5}
: We take
\delta_{l},\delta_{u}
as given from step
m=3,4
. First, note that
\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}^{>}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})\leq 0
. Applying Lemma
7.2
(d) with
R=\max\{\delta_{u},\eqref{eq_proof:choiceR}\}
gives the estimate (
F.12
) on the remainder in (
7.7
) for which we additionally observe
g(\beta_{0}/(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}))\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(\delta_{l},\delta_{u})
. Moreover, we have (
F.6
) such that we obtain from decomposition (
7.7
) for some constant
C_{17}=C_{17}(\delta_{l},\delta_{u})
,

\displaystyle\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(C_{2}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{17}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{17}n^{2/3}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\frac{2C_{2}\sqrt{n}}{2}F_{n,(2C_{17}/C_{2})n^{\gamma/6}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{17}n^{2/3}+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\right),

where the last step combines
C_{2}nf(\alpha_{0}/(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}))
and
nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}
as it was already done in step
m=2
. From the final expression, we then argue as in (1) and (2) in step
m=2
and obtain that this term tends to zero as
n\to\infty
.

F.2
Proof of Lemma
7.2

Let
(Y_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
be a sequence of random variables with
\mathbb{E}[|Y_{n}|]\leq C\psi_{n}
. Then by Markov’s inequality, we can choose
C^{\prime}
larg enough such that

\mathbb{P}\left(|Y_{n}|>C^{\prime}\psi_{n}\right)\leq\frac{C\psi_{n}}{C^{\prime}\psi_{n}}<\epsilon,

and for the set
B_{n}=\{|Y_{n}|\leq C^{\prime}\psi_{n}\}
we have
|Y_{n}|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq C^{\prime}\psi_{n}
and
\mathbb{P}(B_{n}^{c})<\epsilon
. In what follows, we will only construct moment bounds for all random variables in (a)-(d). The family of sets
(B_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
in the statement can then be constructed by the procedure mentioned before.

First of all, we observe that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\theta_{\rho}\leq Ln^{-\gamma/2}}\left|H_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left[\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{2}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}.

By a Taylor expansion, we find for
n\geq n_{0}=n_{0}(L,\alpha_{0})
large enough that the square bracket is bounded by
C_{\alpha_{0}}(L)/\sqrt{n}
, uniformly in
|\theta_{\alpha}|\leq L/\sqrt{n}
. Moreover, by applying iterative conditioning and Corollary
B.2
and subsequently the bound (
B.1
) on the transition density,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sqrt{n}

and putting together both bounds gives the claim. The estimate for
H_{n,\gamma}^{>}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})
follows analogously.
∎

First, we obtain the uniform estimate

\displaystyle\sup_{|\theta_{\rho}|\leq Ln^{-\gamma/2}}\left|H_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})-H_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(0,\theta_{\alpha})\right|\leq\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|S_{n,\gamma}^{1}+\frac{1}{2}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|S_{n,\gamma}^{2},

with

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right),

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right).

Note that
S_{n,\gamma}^{j}\geq 0
for
j=1,2
. The claim then follows once we prove the moment bound
\mathbb{E}_{0}[S_{n,\gamma}^{j}]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\sqrt{n}
for
j=1,2
. To this aim, we first bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}\}}.\end{split}

(F.16)

Replacing
K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}
with
Ln^{-\gamma}
in (
E.2
) gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+Ln^{-\gamma/2}\}}\right]\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L}{\sqrt{k}}.

The same bound holds true for
X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
replaced by
X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
such that in total we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[S_{n,\gamma}^{1}\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\sqrt{n}.

To find the required moment bound for
S_{n,\gamma}^{2}
, one first applies (
F.16
). Replacing
K\psi_{n}^{\gamma}
with
Ln^{-\gamma/2}
in the estimates (
E.3
) and (
E.4
) then gives

\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[S_{n,\gamma}^{2}\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}L\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}K\sqrt{n}

and the first bound in Lemma
7.2
(b) follows. The second one can be shown analogously.
∎

The claim follows, once we establish

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}_{0}}\left(\sqrt{n}\right)

(F.17)

and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\beta_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right|=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}_{0}}\left(\sqrt{n}\right)

(F.18)

In what follows, we only give a detailed proof for (
F.17
) as (
F.18
) follows analogously. By the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|\leq T_{1}+T_{2},

where

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle:=\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle:=\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|.

In what follows, we establish the desired stochastic order for each summand seperately. For
T_{1}
we first note that the sum inside the absolute value is a sum over martingale increments. Then using Markov’s and Jensen’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(T_{1}>C\sqrt{n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{1}{C\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{0}[T_{1}^{2}]}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\frac{1}{C\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right)^{2}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\Big]\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{1}{C\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{4}}{(\Delta_{n}^{\gamma})^{2}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}}{C}\longrightarrow 0

for
C\to\infty
, where the last step uses Corollary
B.2
. This proves that
T_{1}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}_{0}}(\sqrt{n})
and finishes the first part.
For the analysis of
T_{2}
, we note
\mathbb{P}_{0}(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}\gamma}=\rho_{0})=0
and only work on
B_{k}:=\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}\}
. Decomposing
1=\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}
reveals by the triangle inequality

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]\right|\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]\right|.\end{split}

(F.19)

Subsequently, both summmands will be bounded seperately. The second expectation on the right-hand side of (
F.19
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\infty}\left(\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\beta_{0}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\frac{y-\rho_{0}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)^{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{2\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}^{2}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\int_{-(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\left[\bigg(y-\frac{(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\bigg)^{2}\hskip-2.84544pt-\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]\varphi(y)dy.

where
\varphi(y)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp(-y^{2}/2)
is the Gaussian density. Now, using
x\mapsto(x^{2}+1)\exp(-x^{2}/4)
is bounded together with a Gaussian-type tail inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{-(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\left(\left(y-\frac{(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\right)^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{\beta_{0}^{2}}\right)\varphi(y)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\int_{-(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\int_{-(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\int_{-(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right).

This bound will turn out to be sufficient for later purposes. For the first expectation on the right-hand side of (
F.19
), we find

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\left(\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\alpha_{0}^{2}\left|\int_{-\infty}^{(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\varphi(y)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\alpha_{0}^{2}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\int_{-\infty}^{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\left(\left(y-\frac{2(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})}{\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\right)^{2}-1\right)\varphi(y)dy\right|,

where again
\varphi
denotes the Gaussian density. As
\int_{\mathbb{R}}y^{2}\varphi(y)dy=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\varphi(y)dy
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})/\sqrt{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\varphi(y)dy=-\int_{(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})/\sqrt{\beta_{0}^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\varphi(y)dy.

Then, as
\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq 0
, we can proceed as for the second summand in (
F.19
). Finally,

\left|\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\left.\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right]-\beta_{0}^{2}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right).

By Corollary
B.1
we then obtain

\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\right]\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sqrt{n}

which yields
T_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}_{0}}(1)
.
∎

We prove the statement for
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})
, the other one follows analogously. First of all, we obtain the estimate

R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})=\sum_{j=1}^{4}R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,j}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}),

where with the abbreviation
D_{k}=(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})
and suppression of the argument
(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})
for shorter displays,

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt\cdot\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\right)

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,3}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}{\alpha_{0}}\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,4}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left[\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\left.-\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\right].

In what follows, we will prove

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,j}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,j}(\theta_{\rho}),

(F.20)

for
j=1,3,4
with

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,j}(\theta_{\rho})=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

(F.21)

In case
j=2
we obtain for
\theta_{\alpha}\leq R
with
R>0
the bound (
F.20
) with

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}(\theta_{\rho})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\bigg(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}-\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\left.+\exp\left(\frac{1}{(\alpha_{0}+R)^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\left(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho-\theta_{\rho}\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\right)

and the constant then additionally depends on
R
. In case
\theta_{\alpha}>R
, we obtain

R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}.

The required stochastic order of
\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,j}
for
j=1,3,4
can then be derived analogously as for the remainder terms
\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}
and
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}
in the proof of Lemma
4.1
in
Brutsche and Rohde 2026
and that for
j=2
following the lines of
\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)},\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)},\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}
and
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}
in the same reference, both times working on the set
A_{n}
given in (
7.2
). We omit the details.

\mathbf{\bullet}\ R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}
.

First, we note that

\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\right)=-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

This entails

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{2}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

\mathbf{\bullet}\ R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}
.

Recall the abbreviation

D_{k}=(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}).

Using that on the event
\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}
