1-\frac{|\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}|}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\leq 1-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)\leq 1+\frac{|\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}|}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}

and the inequality
\log(1+x)\leq x
for
x>-1
yields

R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})+R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right),

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})&:=\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\right|\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)\\
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})&:=\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\left|\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)\right|.\end{split}

(F.22)

We are going to treat these terms seperately. For the first one, a direct evaluation in the frist step reveals

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=2\left|\frac{\beta_{0}\theta_{\alpha}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}-\frac{\alpha_{0}\theta_{\beta}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{2\beta_{0}|\theta_{\alpha}|}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}+\frac{2\alpha_{0}|\theta_{\beta}|}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})(\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{2|\theta_{\alpha}|}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}+\frac{2|\theta_{\beta}|}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}.

Using that
|x-1|\leq|\log(x)|
for
0<x\leq 1
and
|x-1|\leq(x^{2}-1)/2
for
x>1
, and recalling
g(x)=|\log(x)|+|x^{2}-1|/2
, we thus find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq 2\left(g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})\right)\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq 2\left(g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+g\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right)\left[\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}-\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\right.\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\left.\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}\right].\end{split}

(F.23)

For the discussion of
R_{n}^{\leq,2,2}
, let
R>0
and assume that
\theta_{\alpha}\leq R<\infty
.
Then, we have for an intermediate value
\xi_{k}(n,\theta_{\alpha})
between
\alpha_{0}^{-2}
and
(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{-2}
which are both lower bounded by
(\alpha_{0}+R)^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\left|\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\left|\frac{1}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}\right|\frac{2D_{k}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\exp\left(-\frac{2\xi_{k}(n,\theta_{\alpha})}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|\frac{2D_{k}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+R)^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha_{0}}(R)\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|\exp\left(-\frac{1}{(\alpha_{0}+R)^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right),

where the last step uses that
x\mapsto x\exp(-(\alpha_{0}+R)^{-2}x/2)
is bounded. The required bound can then be obtained as in the second step of (
F.23
).

In case
\theta_{\alpha}>R
, we directly bound the logarithm in the definition of
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}
using its monotonicity. On the event
\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}
we have
D_{k}\geq 0
and thus find on this event

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})D_{k}\right)}\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{2}{1-\frac{|\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}|}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}}\right)=d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}},

which summed over
k=1,\dots,n
directly gives the bound
nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
.

\mathbf{\bullet}\ R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,3}
.

By the mediant inequality, we have for
a,b,c,d>0
,

\frac{a+b}{c+d}\leq\max\left\{\frac{a}{c},\frac{b}{d}\right\},

such that

\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\leq\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|+\left|\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right|

and in total

\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}{\alpha_{0}}\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\leq 2\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|+2\left|\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right|.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,3}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left|\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right|+\left|\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot 2\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

\mathbf{\bullet}\ R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,4}
.

First, we observe that

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\beta_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}\right)\left(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\left(\frac{1}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}\right).

For
a,b\geq 0
we have

\left|1-ab\right|\leq\left|1-\min\{a,b\}^{2}\right|+\left|1-\max\{a,b\}^{2}\right|=\left|1-a^{2}\right|+\left|1-b^{2}\right|,

such that we obtain

\displaystyle R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right|+\left|\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-1\right|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{0}^{2}}+\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}\beta_{0}}+\frac{1}{\beta_{0}^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}.

∎

F.3
Proof of auxiliary results

Lemma F.2
.

For all
x,y>0
we have

f(x,y):=\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)y^{2}+\log(y)-\frac{1}{2}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\leq 0

with equality if and only if
y=1/x
.

It is clear that
f(x,1/x)=0
by a direct calculation. Now fix
x>0
and define
g_{x}(y)=f(x,y)
. Then,

g_{x}^{\prime}(y)=-x^{2}y+\frac{1}{y}=\frac{1}{y}\left(1-x^{2}y^{2}\right).

From this it follows that
g_{x}^{\prime}(y)>0
for
y<1/x
and
g_{x}^{\prime}(y)<0
for
y>1/x
, hence the statement of the lemma.
∎

The next result establishes important properties of the function
F_{n,C}
given in (
F.10
). In Figure
4
, this function is illustrated for different values of
n
.

Fig 4:
The function
F_{n_{j},C}
for
C=7
and
n_{1}=100
(blue),
n_{2}=250
(red) and
n_{3}=500
(orange). The locatio of the corresponding local maxima are depicted on the x-acis in the respective color.

Lemma F.3
.

Let
C>0
and

F_{n,C}(x)=\sqrt{n}\left(\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)\right)+C\left|\log(x)\right|+\frac{C}{2}\left|x^{2}-1\right|,\quad x>0.

Then, the following hold true:

(a)

\sup_{x>0}\sqrt{n}F_{n,C_{n}}(x)\leq\frac{2C_{n}^{2}}{1-1/\sqrt{2}}
for every
C_{n}
satisfying
C_{n}<\sqrt{n/2}
.

(b)

Let
L>6C
and
n
large enough such that
(2CL+L^{2}+CL^{2}/\sqrt{n})/\sqrt{n}<C
. Then, we have

\sqrt{n}F_{n,C}(x)\leq-\frac{CL}{9}\quad\textrm{ for every }x\notin\left[\frac{1}{1+L/\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{1-L/\sqrt{n}}\right].

(c)

For any
0<\delta<1
and
C_{n}=o(\sqrt{n})
there exists a constant
c=c(\delta,C)>0
and
n_{0}\in\mathbb{N}
such that for every
n\geq n_{0}
,

F_{n,C_{n}}(x)\leq-c\sqrt{n}\quad\textrm{ for all }x\notin[1-\delta,1+\delta].

We start with part (a) and first note that
F_{n,C_{n}}(1)=0
and

F_{n,C_{n}}(x)=\begin{cases}(\sqrt{n}-C_{n})\log(x)-(\sqrt{n}+C_{n})(x^{2}-1)/2,&\textrm{ if }0<x\leq 1,\\
(\sqrt{n}+C_{n})\log(x)-(\sqrt{n}-C_{n})(x^{2}-1)/2,&\textrm{ if }x>1.\end{cases}

From this, we directly get

F_{n,C_{n}}^{\prime}(x)=\begin{cases}(\sqrt{n}-C_{n})\frac{1}{x}-(\sqrt{n}+C_{n})x,&\textrm{ if }0<x\leq 1,\\
(\sqrt{n}+C_{n})\frac{1}{x}-(\sqrt{n}-C_{n})x,&\textrm{ if }x>1,\end{cases}

such that for
C_{n}<\sqrt{n}
,
F_{n,C_{n}}^{\prime}(x)=0
is satisfied for

x_{\leq 1,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}}\quad\textrm{ and }\quad x_{>1,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}}.

One easily verifies that
F_{n,C_{n}}^{\prime\prime}(x)<0
for
C<\sqrt{n}
in which case both
x_{\leq 1,n}
and
x_{>1,n}
are local maxima. As it is easy to see that for
C_{n}<\sqrt{n}
, we have
F_{n,C_{n}}(x)\rightarrow-\infty
for both
x\to 0
and
x\to\infty
and
F_{n,C_{n}}(x)\rightarrow 0
for
x\to 1
, so these are in fact global maxima. Inserting then yields with
\log(1+x)\leq x

\displaystyle F_{n,C_{n}}(x_{\leq 1,n})

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{2}\log\left(\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}\right)-\frac{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}{2}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}-1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{2}\log\left(\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}\right)+C_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{2}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}-1\right)+C_{n}

\displaystyle=C_{n}\left(1-\frac{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}\right).

In particular,

\sqrt{n}F_{n,C_{n}}(x_{\leq 1,n})\leq\frac{2C_{n}^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{n}+C_{n}}\leq 2C_{n}^{2}.

Along these lines, we also obtain for
C_{n}<\sqrt{n/2}

\sqrt{n}F_{n,C_{n}}(x_{>1,n})\leq\frac{2C_{n}^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{n}-C_{n}}\leq\frac{2C_{n}^{2}}{1-1/\sqrt{2}}

and finish the proof of part (a). For part (b), we first observe that

\frac{1}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}}-x_{\leq 1,n}^{2}=\frac{2(C-L)+(2CL-L^{2}+CL^{2}/\sqrt{n})/\sqrt{n}}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}(\sqrt{n}+C)}<0

under the conditions in part (b). Since
F_{n,C}^{\prime}(x)\geq 0
for
x\leq x_{\leq 1,n}
, meaning that
F_{n,C}(x)
is monotonously increasing on
(0,x_{\leq 1,n}]
, we find for any
x\leq 1/(1+L/\sqrt{n})
that

\displaystyle F_{n,C}(x)

\displaystyle\leq F_{n,C}\left(\frac{1}{1+L/\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\sqrt{n}-C\right)\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{1+L/\sqrt{n}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}}-1\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt-C\left(\frac{1}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}}-1\right)

A second order Taylor expansion of
x\mapsto\log(x)-(x^{2}-1)/2
in
x_{0}=1
with intermediate value
\xi
between
1
and
x
yields

\log(x)-\frac{1}{2}\left(x^{2}-1\right)=-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}\right)(x-1)^{2}\leq-\frac{(x-1)^{2}}{2}.

This entails

\displaystyle F_{n,C}(x)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\sqrt{n}-C}{2}\left(\frac{1}{1+L/\sqrt{n}}-1\right)^{2}-C\left(\frac{1}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}}-1\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{L}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{2}}\left(\frac{L}{2}-2C-\frac{3CL}{2\sqrt{n}}\right)\leq-\frac{CL}{9\sqrt{n}},

which proves the second assertion for
x<(1+L/\sqrt{n})^{-1}
. The claim for
x>(1-L/\sqrt{n})^{-1}
follows along the same lines by first observing
x_{>1,n}^{2}-(1-L/\sqrt{n})^{-2}>0
and then verifying
F_{n,C}(x)\leq-4CL/\sqrt{n}
for
x>(1-L/\sqrt{n})^{-1}
. This finishes (b) and we start to prove (c). From the form of
F_{n,C_{n}}^{\prime}(x)
, we obtain that
F_{n,C_{n}}(x)
is monotonically increasing for
x\in(0,x_{\leq 1,n}]\supset(0,1-\delta)
and monotonically decreasing for
x\in[x_{>1,n},\infty)\supset(1+\delta,\infty)
. The inclusions are valid for
n
sufficiently large because
x_{\leq 1,n},x_{>1,n}\rightarrow 1
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, proving
F_{n,C_{n}}(1\pm\delta)\leq-c\sqrt{n}
for
n
large enough suffices to establish (c). For
1-\delta
this follows from

F_{n,C_{n}}(1-\delta)=\sqrt{n}\left(\log(1-\delta)-\frac{1}{2}\left((1-\delta)^{2}-1\right)\right)-C_{n}\left(\log(1-\delta)+\frac{1}{2}\left((1-\delta)^{2}-1\right)\right)

together with the fact that
\log(1-\delta)-((1-\delta)^{2}-1)/2\leq-c^{\prime}(\delta)
for an appropricate constant
c^{\prime}(\delta)>0
and
C_{n}=o(\sqrt{n})
. For
1+\delta
, the claim follows analogously.
∎

The next Lemma provides two alternative estimates to the decomposition (
7.7
), one that is useful for
\theta_{\beta}\approx-\beta_{0}
and the other for
\theta_{\beta}\to\infty
. These are used in steps
m=3,4
in the proof of Proposition
3.3
in Subsection
F.1
.

Recall the notation of Section
7
, the functions
f(x)=\log(x)-(x^{2}-1)/2
and
g(x)=|\log(x)|+|x^{2}-1|/2
, the constant
d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
from Lemma
7.2
(d) and the set
A_{n}
given in (
7.2
) together with the constant
\zeta
in the definition of this set. Recall that all constants may depend on the globally chosen parameters
\zeta,\xi,\Gamma
of this set
A_{n}
and the true levels of volatility
\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}
.

Lemma F.4
.

Let
\epsilon,R>0
. Then there exists a sequence of sets
(B_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
with
B_{n}\subset A_{n}
and
\mathbb{P}_{0}(B_{n}^{c})<\epsilon
together with constants
C_{1},C_{2}=C_{2}(R)>0
such that the following statements hold true:

(i)

There exists
\delta_{l}\in(0,1]
such that for all
\theta_{\beta}<-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l}
and
\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(C_{1}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{2}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+C_{2}n^{2/3}+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[2\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}.

(ii)

There exists
\delta_{u}>0
such that for
\theta_{\beta}>-\beta_{0}+\delta_{u}
and
\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\left(C_{1}nf\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+C_{2}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)+C_{2}n^{2/3}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\theta_{\alpha}>R\}}\bigg)+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}.

We begin the proof by decomposing

\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\ell_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})=\sum_{j=1}^{5}S_{n,\gamma}^{j}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}),

where with the abbreviation
D_{k}=(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})
and suppressing the argument
(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})
for shorter displays,

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{1}

\displaystyle:=\ell_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\alpha_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{3}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}-1\right)\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{4}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{1+\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}{\alpha_{0}}\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.\left.-\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}{\beta_{0}}\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.\left.-\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)^{2}\right)\right].

In what follows, we treat these terms independently of one another. Let us now define
B_{n}
as the intersection of the following three events:
B_{1,n}=
the set for which the bound (
7.5
) is valid,
B_{2,n}=
the set in Lemma
7.2
(also called
B_{n}
in there) and the event

B_{3,n}:=\left\{\max_{0\leq t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}X_{t}-X_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>1\right\}.

Recall that
\limsup_{n}\mathbb{P}_{0}(B_{1,n}^{c})=0
as discussed right before (
7.5
) and
\limsup_{n}\mathbb{P}_{0}(B_{2,n}^{c})=0
which is part of the statement of Lemma
7.2
. To see that
\mathbb{P}_{0}(B_{3,n})\rightarrow 1
, we employ the Dambis–Dubins–Schwarz theorem (Theorem
19.4
in
Kallenberg 2021
). Indeed, let be an independent Brownian motion
B
(possibly on an extension of the original probability space, we also denote the measure on this extension with
\mathbb{P}_{0}
) such that
X_{t}=B_{\langle X\rangle_{t}}
for all
t\geq 0
almost surely. Then,

\max_{0\leq t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}X_{t}-X_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}=\max_{0\leq t\leq\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}B_{t}-B_{\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}.

Using the distributional identity
\max_{0\leq s\leq t}B_{s}-B_{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathcal{D}}}{{=}}|B_{t}|
(Proposition
14.13
in
Kallenberg 2021
), we obtain by conditioning

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\max_{0\leq t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}X_{t}-X_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq 1\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\max_{0\leq t\leq\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}B_{t}-B_{\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\leq 1\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left.\max_{0\leq t\leq\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}B_{t}-B_{\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\leq 1\right|\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left.\big|B_{\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\big|\leq 1\right|\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{0}\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}}\right]\leq\frac{2}{\min\{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}\}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}}\rightarrow 0,

where the last inequality used that

\langle X\rangle_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}=\int_{0}^{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\sigma_{\rho_{0},\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(X_{t})^{2}dt\geq\min\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}.

We now prove assertion (i) by estimating each
S_{n,\gamma}^{j}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}
for
j=1,\dots,5
seperately.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{1}}
.

We have the decomposition

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}(0,\theta_{\alpha})+T_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})+R_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left(H_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})-\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\right)+R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}),

where
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}
is defined in the proof of Lemma
7.2
(d), does not depend on
\theta_{\beta}
and is shown to satisfy

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}>\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2}R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}Cg\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)n^{1/2+\gamma/6}.

The same bound is valid for
\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}(H_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})-\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha})
by Lemma
7.2
(c). By Lemma
7.1
applied to
a=\zeta\sqrt{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}/2
and
b=0
, we obtain

\overline{H}_{n,\gamma}(0,\theta_{\alpha})\leq n\frac{\xi\zeta}{2\max\{\alpha_{0}^{2},\beta_{0}^{2}\}}f\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)

and
\sup_{\theta_{\rho}}R_{n,\gamma}^{1}(\theta_{\rho})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq Cn^{1/2+\gamma/6}
according to (
7.5
). All these upper bounds appear on the right-hand side of the statement.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{2}}
.

This term is the same as
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}+R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2})
in the proof of Lemma
7.2
(d) where also the definition of
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1},R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2}
is given in (
F.22
). To bound the first one, we apply the inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}>-\alpha_{0},\theta_{\beta}>-\beta_{0}}\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\right|\leq 2

(F.24)

and then follow the treatment of
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}
in the proof of Lemma
7.2
(d). The reasoning for
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2}
does not change at all compared to the proof of Lemma
7.2
(d) and in total we obtain

\sup_{\theta_{\rho}}S_{n,\gamma}^{2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq C(R)n^{1/2+\gamma/6}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right)

in case
\theta_{\alpha}\leq R
and obtain the bound
nd_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}
in case
\theta_{\alpha}>R
.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{3}}
.

This term is already present on the right-hand side of the assertion.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{4}}
.

As for
S_{n,\gamma}^{2}
we obtain
S_{n,\gamma}^{4}\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}(S_{n,\gamma}^{4,1}+S_{n,\gamma}^{4,2})
, where the terms
S_{n,\gamma}^{4,1},S_{n,\gamma}^{4,2}
replace
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1},R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,2}
from the proof of
S_{n,\gamma}^{2}
and are given as

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{4,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{4,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left|\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)\right|,

where
D_{k}=(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})
. The term
S_{n,\gamma}^{4,1}
can then be dealt with as
R_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,2,1}
in the proof of Lemma
7.2
(d) by again applying bound (
F.24
). For
S_{n,\gamma}^{4,2}
we observe that the factor infront of the sum is uniformly in
\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}
bounded by one. Moreover,
(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{-2}\geq\delta_{l}^{-2}
and by choosing
\delta_{l}\in(0,1]
small enough such that
\delta_{l}^{-2}\geq\beta_{0}^{-2}
we obtain by observing
D_{k}\geq 0
on the event
\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{0}\}

S_{n,\gamma}^{4,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq 2\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)

and we can then follow the follow the lines of
S_{n,\gamma}^{4,1}
. In total we then obtain

S_{n,\gamma}^{4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{5}}
.

In this part, the event
B_{3,n}
is used in a somewhat surprising way. First of all we drop negative summands and bound

S_{n,\gamma}^{5}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})+S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})+S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})+S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta}),

where

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\left.+\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left[\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\left(\left(\frac{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\alpha_{0}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}}{\beta_{0}}\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right),

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}{\beta_{0}}\right).

For
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}
, we note that for
\delta_{l}\leq 1
,

\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}>-\alpha_{0}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l})}\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}{\alpha_{0}}\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}}\right).

Next, we have

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\left(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right)^{2}

and the same with
k
replaced by
(k-1)
. This means that in total

S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta}),

where
\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}
is given in (
F.21
) and satisfies
\Upsilon_{n,\gamma}^{\leq,1}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}n^{1/2+\gamma/6}
. For
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}
we note that

\sup_{\theta_{\beta}\in(-\beta_{0},-\beta_{0}+\delta_{l})}\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right)

and then proceed as for
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}
to obtain

S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right)n^{1/2+\gamma/6}.

We now turn to
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}
which is the most interesting part of the proof. This term is positive for
\theta_{\beta}<0
and this positivity cannot be compensated within the same regime, i.e. with other indices
k
for which
k\in I_{up}:=\{1\leq k\leq n:X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\leq X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}
. The idea to solve this issue is to identify possible (negative) compensators and there are two different types:

\bullet

For each
k
with
X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
we obtain the corresponding negative summand
-\log(\beta_{0}/(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}))
which is part of
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}
.

\bullet

From
S_{n,\gamma}^{3}
, we already have the summand

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

(F.25)

which in an application of this lemma is seen to compensated for by the negative term

-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{0}^{2}}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}})^{2}}{\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}\beta_{0}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}}

that is also part of
S_{n,\gamma}^{3}
. In principle, adding this term and two times (
F.25
) would also result in a negative term with high probability.

Now, the crucial observation is that on the event
B_{n}
, we can construct for each path an injective mapping

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{up}\rightarrow\left\{k:X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\right\}\cup\left\{k:X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\right\},\end{split}

(F.26)

where especially
B_{3,n}
plays an important role: For each
k\leq n-1
with
k\in I_{up}
we obviously have either
X_{(k+1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
or
X_{(k+1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
and map
k\mapsto k+1
. For
k=n
we have no successor and argue with the path properties of
(X_{t})_{0\leq t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
and our particular choice of
B_{n}
. On this event, the difference of the running maximum of
(X_{t})_{0\leq t\leq n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
and
X_{n\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}
is at least one. Since
|X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}|\leq(\Delta_{n}^{\gamma})^{4/9}
on
A_{n}\supset B_{n}
for two consecutive observations, this implies that for
n
large enough, there exists a smallest index
k_{0}\leq n-2
such that three consecutive observations lie above
\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
, i.e.
X_{k_{0}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{(k_{0}+1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{(k_{0}+2)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
. In particular,
k_{0}+1\notin I_{up}
such that we can map
n\mapsto k_{0}+2
(see Figure
5
for an illustration).

Fig 5:
An illustration of discrete realization, linearly interpolated. To construct the injection (
F.26
), each upcrossing (red) is mapped to its successor, except of the last segment. Here, we chose one of the earlier segments above the threshold that has no preceeding upcrossing. Such a segment always exists if there are three consecutive points above the threshold.

Using the above construction of an injective mapping in (
F.26
) reveals

\displaystyle S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}<\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle=-S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\beta})+\log\left(\frac{\beta_{0}}{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}<X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\}}.

Now it is clear that
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}+S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}
appears on the right-hand side of the statement and the proof of part (i) is finished.

We finally turn to part (ii) that is actually easier to prove than (i). Here,
S_{n,\gamma}^{j}
can be dealt with the same as in (i) for
j=1,2,3
and we discuss the remaining two cases.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{4}}
.

We observe that as
D_{k}=(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})(X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})\geq 0
on the event that
X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}
,

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}-(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{(\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta})^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}D_{k}\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta_{0}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}(X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}-\rho_{0}-\theta_{\rho})D_{k}\right)}\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{2}{1-\frac{|\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}|}{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}}\right)=d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}},

such that

S_{n,\gamma}^{4}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\leq d_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho},X_{k\Delta_{n}^{\gamma}}>\rho_{0}+\theta_{\rho}\}},

which appear on the right-hand side of the statement.

\bullet\ \mathbf{S_{n,\gamma}^{5}}
.

Recall
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,j}
,
j=1,2,3,4
, from the treatment of
S_{n,\gamma}^{5}
in (i). Nothing has to be changed in the discussion of
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,1}
and
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,3}\leq 0
for
\delta_{u}>0
. Adding
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}+S_{n,\gamma}^{5,4}
and observing that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta_{\alpha}}\sup_{\theta_{\beta}>\delta_{u}-\beta_{0}}\log\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}+\beta_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}+\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\frac{\beta_{0}+\theta_{\beta}}{\beta_{0}}\right)\leq C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right),

once can proceed as for
S_{n,\gamma}^{5,2}
in (i). In total, this gives

S_{n,\gamma}^{5}(\theta_{\rho},\theta_{\alpha},\theta_{\beta})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{n}}C_{\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}}\left(1+g\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{0}+\theta_{\alpha}}\right)\right)n^{1/2+\gamma/6},

which appears on the right-hand side of the statement.

∎
